# Light Remote Worklog — Project Evolution and Durable Checkpoints

Status: chronological record
Date: 2026-10-30
Scope: durable changes in architecture, accepted evidence, product direction and blockers

This is one **append-only chronological journal**. It is not a handoff per chat and never replaces Git, test receipts or current runtime inspection. A checkpoint is a short mutable cursor; history stays here and in original source documents.

## 2026-10-30 — A/B V2 approval TTL decision: 5 minutes; legacy V1 unchanged
Before proceeding with P2-I1/I2 implementation, checked `operator-host/device-pairing-registry.mjs`: real legacy A code defaults to `DEFAULT_PAIRING_CODE_TTL_MS = 3 * 60 * 1000` and currently allows configured bounds of 30 seconds to 10 minutes. Owner considered 3 vs 5 minutes for the *new* Agent-initiated V2. Chosen product design is **300 seconds (5 minutes) fixed for V2 pending approval only**, calculated by server from exact request creation. The six-character immutable device Short ID remains visible indefinitely, the five-character temporary A suffix and independently issued B expire with the same pending request, with no timeout extension from polling/page reload/failed entry. One-time use, hard approval deadline, exact device/actor/request binding, rate limits, revocation and 24-hour approved A/B lease are mandatory. Legacy V1 TTL remains three minutes for client compatibility. This is a design/documentation decision; V2 protocol and timeout are NOT yet deployed or wired to live runtime.

## 2026-10-30 — A/B V2 Refresh/Recovery architecture retained from V1
Owner flagged that legacy Wall **Refresh A code** is an essential self-recovery mechanism when an Agent cannot find/consume the code, client time appears inconsistent or the connection is interrupted. Added source-only V2 design contract to Gold integrated plan §14 and acceptance ledger A04. When STANDBY, Wall always shows stable six-character Short ID, can refresh device/server status and copy Short ID, and offers **explicit manual V1 pairing fallback** when V2 target resolution is unavailable. When an exact V2 request is PENDING, owner-controlled Refresh atomically invalidates old A suffix + B and creates an attempt generation with fresh 300-second server deadline, while the bound Agent's next Helper poll receives the replacement B; no auto TTL extension from polling/page reload, no cross-Agent request mix, no local code minting while server offline, and no A/B approval bypass. These requirements are **not implemented or deployed**; V1 runtime remains 180 seconds. CI/Git documentation sync still required before considering this log committed.

## 2026-10-30 — P2 owner hotfix: independently scoped A/B 24-hour timers per Agent

Owner screenshot of Windows 11 Local Wall 5491 (agent rc49) showed a single `Agent A/B access` clock still counting old expiry after a fresh owner's B approval. The initial approach (select newest grant on whole device) was explicitly refined by owner: **multiple Agents each need their OWN countdown**; neither close nor reset other Agent grants. Source root cause: backend `activeForDevice()` returned first active grant and Wall rendered one single countdown. Replaced with canonical read-only `activeGrantsForDevice` all independent agent scopes, safe signed `status.access.activeGrants[]`, and Wall per-Agent DOM timers; old singular field retained for compatibility. Same Agent B re-approval replaces only its own old grant in one durable persisted snapshot; another Agent or Team member is untouched. Owner B approval invalidates cached Wall status immediately and signed approval event forces a read. No reset on A creation, pending, OAuth or status polling.

New dedicated fixture PASS verifies A 90 seconds left while B starts 24h, B counts independently through A expiry, same-Agent reapproval, Team Agent, restart persistence, signed HTTP status minimal metadata, actual Local Wall B approval cache invalidation, and DOM multi-clock. Updated obsolete V1 fixture from shared approval expectation to independent per-Agent B approvals, all related V1 pairing regressions PASS. This is SOURCE ONLY in VPS-ARM Gold worktree: Windows rc49 in screenshot and Gold UAT/production remain untouched. Source commit `31c0e702ec590b9148f508c621f0d0b727a2b099` pushed; HEAD=origin and source↔docs checkpoint PASS, local suites and 330 docs links PASS. GitHub Actions push-run NOT VERIFIED from current connector; two Vercel checks pending at first inspection. Next controlled candidate/native Wall acceptance; then return to P2-I2h issuance/grant reconciliation.

Owner supplied screenshots after linking Windows: Wall at `127.0.0.1:5491` showed `Short ID (Device ID) —`, online device name `DESKTOP-VA16D27`. GoldABV2 account screenshot showed that hostname Main/online and another QA row; WinForms Tray still displayed the previously built 500x740 white popover. Owner explicitly requires **OS-native Windows tray menu**, in this exact order: `Hostname - Short ID : XXXXXX`; status `Connected` / disconnected/etc.; Open Light Remote (open the unified Desktop V4 app); Open Wall; Open Fleet (**only when THIS device is Main in account**); Manage Account; Restart; Stop; Check Update (1) if a verified update is pending; Exit. Owner subsequently added **Link / Relink** as an explicit item, placed between Manage Account and Restart. No separate custom popover should be invoked.

**What was verified LIVE, before this code change:** LXD `light-remote-abv2-lab-20261101`, PostgreSQL `light_remote_gold_dev`: schema version **43**, `lr_gold_device_short_ids` table exists with six assigned rows. The online `DESKTOP-VA16D27` records and `Windows GoldABV2 Desktop V4 QA` had existing Short ID records, with **multiple same-name device rows**, so do NOT generate another ID from the hostname, do NOT overwrite immutable mapping and do NOT manually pick a different device's code. The Wall's `—` is a *read/delivery issue*: old signed Operator `/v1/device-channel/status` lacked `shortId`, while `/v1/device-channel/abv2-pending-preview` returns a Short ID only when its separate explicit allowlist and Owner proof succeed. A Preview 404 or null code previously left the UI as dash. Exact preview failure on this particular physical Windows session was not independently measured; don't claim the allowlist is definitely the cause.

**Source patch on `feat/desktop-v4-api-integration-20261103`:**
- `operator-host/executor.mjs`: read-only, schema43-gated `goldShortIdByDevice({deviceId,accountId})` queries the immutable PG Short ID joined to `lr_devices`, scoped by the **signed** device's owner and exact device ID, excludes revoked. Missing DB/row returns null; no auto-generated/guessed fallback.
- `operator-host/executor-routes-device-channel.mjs`: existing Ed25519 verified `POST /v1/device-channel/status` now returns `shortId` and `isMain`. `isMain` is derived from `accounts.account(binding.accountId).mainDeviceId===ctx.device.deviceId`, NEVER from local Fleet health or a device-supplied flag. Existing A/B V1 423 server lock preserved.
- `device-agent/local-wall.mjs`: R5 frontend accepts Short ID from the signed remote Operator status; a missing/null pending preview no longer overwrites a previously validated code with `—`. Approval preview remains separately protected and does not grant B consent by displaying a code.
- `device-agent/operator-agent.mjs`: CLI `status` now reads signed remote status only if enrolled and connected, projects validated `shortId` and Boolean `isMain`; if remote call fails, safely emits null and false (never a stale/fabricated Main flag). This is needed by native Tray for correct identity/Main visibility. The extra remote status call is bounded by the existing channel timeout; monitor Windows UI responsiveness.
- `client/windows-native/GptOperator.Client/TrayApplicationContext.cs`: **removes all instantiation/activation of `TrayPopoverForm`**; `NotifyIcon.ContextMenuStrip` is now a standard Windows native menu, right-click automatic and left-click same menu; double-click opens installed `desktop-v4/LightRemote.Desktop.exe`. Menu exact labels: top disabled `Environment.MachineName - Short ID : <six-code-or-dashes>`, disabled connection state, separator, Open Light Remote, Open Wall, Open Fleet (only if signed `isMain`; enabled when Fleet healthy), Manage Account (uses correctly configured GoldABV2 `BridgeUrl/account`), **Link / Relink** always visible, Restart, Stop/Start, Check Update (1 only if installed independent updater reports `state=ready` with a different targetVersion), Exit. Link/Relink retains actual Owner-approved native Tray flow; if connected it explains that the Owner must disconnect first, rather than silently stealing a session.
- Old `TrayPopoverForm.cs` may remain as dead source/visual-pilot evidence for historical CI; it is NOT constructed and NOT shown in runtime. Existing updater/task/Agent/Wall remain in the **one** integrated Inno Setup; no second UI installer.
- Tests: `deploy/scripts/selftest-goldabv2-native-tray-menu.mjs` source contract checks native menu, server-signed Short ID/Main, No Popover activation; `operator-host/gold-ab-v2-server-v1-lock.test.mjs` exercises signed metadata + V1 denial; prior `selftest-gold-abv2-wall-short-id-display.mjs` and Gold Lab V1 lock PASS. ## 2026-11-07 — T11-F09 P02A Desktop Owner snapshot/A-B pending polling backpressure

**Owner-authorized VPS-ARM source/CI only.** Baseline source Desktop 9660310015bbb1657f72d49c2546f2277a194451; Gold docs mirror 73572fbc2d4a09d75c6d3ab74f2f206b5e156667. Existing T11-F08B-CI02 Windows complete installer run 42347925595 SUCCESS at older source SHA 6623b1a0ee2b76888f5073ecf273c27819b69598. It is NOT native acceptance of this new P02 patch.

T09 audit performance P02 identifies App.tsx old setInterval polls of Owner snapshot every 20 seconds and ABV2 pending every 8 seconds with NO in-flight guard. Slow native IPC could create overlapping calls, let a stale response overwrite a newer pending request, and consume extra resources. T11-F09 implements a narrow single-flight scheduler in client/desktop-v4/src/lib/scoped-read-poller.ts and integrates it into both app polling loops. One active read per loop; manual refresh after approval invalidates prior pending preview and queues exactly one fresh read after older request settlement. Effect cleanup on Owner/account change stops scheduling and discards late results. Hiding the document invalidates an in-flight projection and suspends background polling; returning to visibility causes immediate fresh read. Unavailable or rejected read publishes typed unavailable (not cached Owner truth) and uses bounded exponential retry capped at 60 seconds; normal cadence resumes on healthy response. Existing native Owner/session/Device policy, B approval and CSRF gates are unchanged; no client token caching, grant retry or authorization bypass. Owner status native verification every 3s, local status every 15s, native tray 5s and native wall_snapshot_current status_inflight behavior are NOT changed by P02A; cross-loop global request budget and performance p95 benchmarking remain open as P02B.

Test source client/desktop-v4/src/lib/scoped-read-poller.test.ts has 5 actual fake-timer async tests: 80-second unresolved snapshot stays one request, forced refresh after Approve queues one read and drops prior result, Owner A unmount cannot write into B, hidden/visible lifecycle, unavailable error/backoff and recovery. Full frontend Vitest suite PASS 77/77 in 16 files, TypeScript typecheck PASS, and new static cross-source gate deploy/scripts/selftest-t11-desktop-poll-backpressure.mjs PASS. Both Windows full installer and Gold GC CI run the source gate, and Desktop V4 Source QA runs the real Vitest tests. No live Windows device or Gold Lab/production touched; no Owner, grant, enrollment, local update feed or settings changes. Gold backend branch remains a doc-only mirror.

**Measurements not claimed:** no physical Windows IPC latency, QPS, CPU, IOPS or Owner signature impact measured; proof is deterministic overlap/late-result fixture only. Next P02A exact Desktop V4 native macOS/Linux QA, Windows full installer and Gold GC/Docs/Truth CI. Then isolate T09 P02B 3s Owner periodic verification vs native Rust wall_snapshot_current, ensuring no weaker revocation fence. P01 UsageRegistry read-side persist and P03 registry N-scan are still open optimization candidates; defer until scoped source fixtures + accounting/Owner boundaries are established.

## 2026-11-07 — T11-F10 P02B Rust Owner verification overlap bounded wait (source candidate)

Owner-authorized source/CI-only patch on VPS-ARM. Baseline Desktop T11-F09-CI01 source a524a2aad3af04f7945af409f0a378889db37c77, Windows full installer run 42350051479 SUCCESS, Web/macOS/Linux run 42350051321 SUCCESS and Gold GC 42350051360 4/4 SUCCESS. Those receipts belong to the PREVIOUS source revision and do not prove this P02B patch.

T09/P02 audit found a concrete native race: Owner status (React every 3 s, Rust HTTPS verification due every 15 s) sets NativeSession.status_inflight=true. OwnerRuntime.wall_snapshot() and wall_snapshot_current() correctly reject access during that verification, but a separate in-progress native Owner snapshot, pending A/B, B Approve or Wall SSO could be rejected solely because periodic verification overlapped its final check. Disabling status_inflight would weaken revocation/refresh protection, so it remains enforced.

New Rust logic (client/desktop-v4/src-tauri/src/owner_login.rs) retains the strict synchronous source-of-truth checks and introduces a bounded 2.5-second async wait (40ms polling intervals, without keeping the Mutex locked over an await) solely for a validated same Owner verification in flight. An anchor freezes native generation, account, token and pre-check last_verified. Completion is accepted only if the original Owner/account/token are unchanged, access deadline remains safely beyond 90-second refresh margin, verification has STOPPED and last_verified has strictly increased (positive HTTPS success). Any revocation, signout, account change, token rotation, refresh, missing fresh verified timestamp or timeout fails closed. No action is retried or duplicated; no Owner credentials are delivered to React or local HTTP. The private Wall SSO, native Owner snapshot, pending B and B Approve use the bounded entry/final read fences; server-side live Owner/device/B verification and existing policy remain authoritative. A lost reply to B mutation remains an UNKNOWN outcome; no automatic second Approve.

Seven native Rust unit tests simulate completion after a short async delay, in-flight final read success, revoked Owner, changed generation/account, rotated token, stuck timeout and clearing inflight without positive HTTPS evidence. deploy/scripts/selftest-t11-owner-verify-wait.mjs checks constants, exact token/generation/time fences, command wiring and presence of those seven actual native tests; Windows full Installer and Gold GC run this source gate. Native cargo test --features desktop-owner-login runs on GitHub macOS+Ubuntu; local source checks PASS. No heavy Rust/native build on VPS-ARM. Exact SHA GitHub Rust compiler/unit tests, Windows native/full installer, Gold GC and Docs/Truth PENDING until commit/runs are inspected. Real Windows Owner/B physical proof remains OPEN. This does NOT optimize the independent 3s Owner React IPC polling, no broad refresh scheduler is changed, and no HTTP round-trip p95/QPS or revocation latency is claimed.

Uses parameterized started_at DESC/job_id DESC keyset, anchorMs high-water mark, <=100 returned records +1 hasMore and total count; flushes pending ledger writes before read. Rejects stale, foreign/cross-kind, relink/revoke during read and malformed/expired cursor. Returned PG metadata is re-sanitized and device-scoped in Operator. Count and keyset are separate PG reads, so concurrent changes/retention may force reload rather than producing a transactionally frozen snapshot.

H2 awaits GoldSessionRedis.flush() before listing present retained sessions and labels source redis_retained_session_window. - Existing lib/gold-session-redis.mjs exposes readonly retentionPolicy(registry) only for the mounted SessionRegistry. Policy is snapshotTtlSeconds=604800 (renewable Redis key TTL), terminalPruneAfterMs=registry.historyMs (runtime-configured terminal row pruning interval), immutableAudit=false. Policy is frozen and excludes Redis namespace, workspace, content, tokens, labels and raw sessions. Invalid or unmounted registry rejects. Existing snapshot EX TTL, SessionRegistry business mutations/GC, keys and schema remain UNCHANGED.
- operator-host/desktop-v4-owner-activity-page.mjs requires canonical session backing policy when claiming redis_retained_session_window (no invented default or implied permanent audit). Revalidates verified active Owner account session and current Owner device membership/revocation after every awaited boundary; unlike previous once-captured device scope, a relink during Redis write fails closed. No new session close or mutation capability.

FeatureScreens.tsx reads the verified Owner session page even with <=200 snapshot sessions and displays separate seven-day renewable Redis snapshot TTL and configured terminal prune days, with explicit 'Not an immutable audit archive'. No browser-level fetch, OAuth token or account selector.
- New lib/gold-session-retention.test.mjs uses fake expiring Redis to prove normal restart recovery of retained terminal sessions; write/flush of pruned tombstone before successful Owner read; no resurrection after restart; expiration of Redis key after inactivity; unmounted policy rejection; no workspace/key leak; fail closed on Redis persistence errors, device owner relink and Owner session logout during await. Expanded Operator, Portal, native projection and FeatureScreens UI tests reject forged/absent policy and source-kind misuse.
- Source guard deploy/scripts/selftest-t11-owner-session-retention-source.mjs binds Gold backing, Owner revocation, Portal, native and UI privacy contract. GitHub Gold GC source gate and Windows full installer source gate trigger on H3 source/tests and run Node regression. The frontend Desktop Web/native QA triggers via client paths.
- Exact detailed decision and future OPTIONAL bounded immutable audit requirements are written in docs/project-book/LIGHT_REMOTE_T11_F15_H3_SESSION_RETENTION_CONTRACT_20261107.md, mirrored in both worktrees. Default is no audit migration, no indefinite retention, no extra personal-data persistence, no release or activation.
